TPIR ROUND 2 (Nov 26, branch, UNRELEASED; owner's playtest list): EPISODE FLOW (tpir/episode.js) = Contestants' Row repeats (MAXROWS 6) until every player has won a bid; a player who wins plays the next game from a no-repeat queue (L0.games + the rest), a guest who wins goes off camera (offCamera = random winnings) and is dealt into a wheel later; players leave the row too (CR replaces any winner's podium); commercial after row 3; one Big Wheel per player who played (player seat 0 + 2 guests, episode.auto=[0] when the CPU/tests play seat 0); wheel winners -> Showcase (a lone player meets a guest); out anywhere (row/wheel/showcase) = goodbye(i) scene (Bob "<name>, thank you for playing", Rich reads Rice-A-Roni + home game); endShow(champ) = new car (newCar scene: car on the turntable + model, winner runs in and jumps) / family / sign-off with host-bye IN FRONT of the celebrating family / credits rolling over the party / tally, only when a real (non-CPU) player won the Showcase; score = cash + prizes. pricingKit ep.out.car (Safe Crackers reports a car won). Test hooks: globalThis.__tpirAuto (games play themselves), __tpirSharp (CR + showcase bots bid close), __tpirWin (players win their wheel); tests/tpir-episode.mjs WxH vs|alone|2p[-win]. CR: names hidden in the bids board until called, COME ON DOWN! above the name (fades where it crosses the face), the call waits up to 2.4 s for that v-dn clip (they were still loading), guest skill lowered (.22+). PRIZES: ST.drawPrizeOn(g,img,cx,footY,bw,bh,{ped,emoji}) in stage.js = the transparent picture trimmed to its visible pixels (visibleBox), no white card (non-alpha photos: gold-framed dark window), optional gold display stand; used in CR, Clock (bigger + the model stands beside it presenting), Plinko, Cliff Hangers, Punch-a-Bunch, goodbye/new car. 46 old Commons photos cut out with rembg's isnet-general-use model (onnx, scratch cut.py; originals assets/originals/prizes/<id>-photo.webp); 13 that cut badly asked of GPT (Drive TALLY-CLAUDE-2026-11-26-REDO-PRIZES.md). PUNCH-A-BUNCH: the question = a big centred card (prize on a stand, name, HIGHER OR LOWER THAN $N?, two big buttons; host/cast hidden meanwhile), the punch = sfx.punch (paper), wrong = buzzer-wrong, small slips aww. VOICES (tpirScript.js -> tpirVoice.js): nm-<slug of shortName> 820 name clips (Bob, "<name>,"), stage.js speak() says "<name>, <rest>" as name clip + the rest's own line (wn-* WITH_NAME rests, or any exact line), "<name>?" = the name; hl-1..120 "Is it higher or lower than $N?" (partsFor: pn-<prize> + hl-N); scx-<showcase>-1/2 bridges, tripd-<trip> write-ups, car-desc-1..4, bob-c-1..6, bye-1/2, wn-*; showcase present() holds each prize through name + description + bridge + one Bob comment. CROWD: tools/gameshow/tpir-crowd.mjs = 12 public fish.audio voices yell lines, ffmpeg layers 6-12 per take -> assets/music/tpir-crowd-<shout|higher|lower|yesno|spinstay|leftright|ooh|aww|cheer>-N + chatter-1 (raw yells cached in local-test-reports/tpir-crowd-raw); murmur() plays only the chatter recording (the slowed applause rumble is gone); K.yell picks the group per moment (Punch ask = higher/lower, keep/punch = yesno, Clock = higher/lower, Big Wheel = spinstay, Plinko aim = leftright). GUIDE: 1P = tapping the show scrolls the guide up (pk) to a PLAY ALONE / VS CPU listing box on the guide (phase 'pick'); the green 1-PLAYER screen is gone. Checked: tpir-episode 390x780 alone (lost at the row; and won a bid -> Range Game -> lost the wheel -> goodbye), 844x390 2p-win (whole path incl. new car + ending), 390x780 vs (commercial, two goodbyes); tests/tpir.mjs all 9 games.

FIGHT NIGHT = PUNCH-OUT (owner: play and feel like NES/SNES Punch-Out, mobile friendly, GPT templates): id fight-night, engine boxing -> src/engines/fightNight.js (old boxing in newGames.js unused), solo 'levels' (career), NET_NATIVE. src/data/poCircuit.js FIGHTERS (11 cast characters: circuit, body type, colours, hp, speed = tell multiplier, dmg, guard high/low/switch/none, move weights jab/hook/upper/body, combo, slip, taunt, armor, special {name,hits,avoid dodge|duck|block,tell}, getup counts, lines, tip), TRAINER rocky-balboa. Foe AI idle -> tell (eye glint, wind-up) -> atk (resolveFoeHit vs your dodge/duck/block windows) -> recover (open; long after a whiff) / quick combo jab; hurt/stun/block/dodge(slip)/taunt/fall/down/getup. You: tap top/bottom half = head/body punch (left/right = hand), swipe L/R dodge, down duck, up star punch (all stars), hold 220 ms = block; keys Z/X/A/S, arrows, C, space. Hit during the tell = COUNTER (+star on hook/upper/special); after a dodge counterT .6 s = COUNTER x1.6 (+star); guard blocks cost a heart; hearts 0 = tired until 2 dodges; stars max 3. HP 100 vs def.hp; knockdowns: ref count .8 s/number, foe rises at getup[downs-1], you mash (8+6*downs taps), 3 in a round = TKO; clock 180 at x3; decision on landed damage + knockdowns. Career: title NEW/CONTINUE (circuit starts 0/3/6/9/10, localStorage sc-po-v1), 3 losses = retired, belt cards, locked opponents beaten join (s.gameRecruits). 2P: same seeded opponent (first 6), turns; board 1 round; online netField both at once, final swap. Art src/engines/po/art.js (TYPES lean/heavy/giant/fast/champ, FOE/ME pose tables, drawFoe/drawMe with reach override, drawRef), slots src/data/poSprites.js (assets/fightnight -> po:, pwa LATER 'po:'; me-p1/p2, foe-<type> or foe-<character id>, referee, arena-<circuit>-tall/wide/fold, corner, logo, title-bg x3, belt, fx-hit, star, heart), templates node tools/po-templates.mjs -> docs/art-for-gpt/fightnight-templates, brief FIGHT-NIGHT-ART.md (+START-HERE row). PENALTY SHOOTOUT (owner: way more fun, like mobile flick games, QoL, templates): engine penalty -> src/engines/penaltyShootout.js (old penaltyKicks.js unused). Metres: goal line z=D, posts +-3.66, bar 2.44; camera behind the ball with yaw toward the goal (free kicks off-centre); swipe -> shotFrom (angle aims, length lifts, speed = power 17+15p m/s, over 1.18 = over the bar, bend = curl accel, velocity solved to hit the aim point), ball sim 240 Hz (drag, curl decay, wind, wall, keeper plane D-.35, posts/bar, net bulge, ground bounce). Keeper: dive to a target (dur by distance), segment + hands hit test, catch/tip/save; CPU guesses early or reads velocity (not the curl) after a reaction delay; human keeper = swipe/tap, flight slowed to .62 when a person keeps. Modes: shootout (5 each, sudden death; board 3), CHALLENGE (levelDef(n): spot/targets/range/free/wind/pressure/curl, walls with cast faces, stars, retries 2, sc-pk-v1 reached/stars/best), TOURNAMENT (3 location teams, keeper faces from the team cast, champion recruits). Replays of goals (rec frames, closer camera, .4 speed). Online: {k:[n,tx,ty,p,c]}, {d:[n,tx,ty,at]}, {r:[n,res]} keeper phone calls. Art src/engines/pk/art.js + src/data/pkSprites.js (assets/penalty -> pk:), templates node tools/pk-templates.mjs, brief PENALTY-ART.md. KINGPIN BOWLING REBUILT (Nov 21, RELEASED as v0.99.39 (owner: "Release all work so far"; test:core, bowl-sim, bowling.mjs 390x780 + 844x390, fuzz, online-native 10 frames OK); owner: "best bowling game ever", Kingpin + Lebowski, score-screen animations like a real alley, upgrade the alley one thing at a time 70s dump -> 90s -> modern -> space, GPT templates). Engine 'bowling' -> src/engines/kingpinBowling.js for BOTH kingpin-bowling and lebowski-bowling (REMAKE_META: one Arcade tile, lebowski arcadeAs kingpin; old movieSports bowling unused); solo kind 'cpu' + NET_NATIVE. bowl/physics.js: ball on an oil pattern (Coulomb slip -> hook -> roll; lane cond len/mid/edge/dry/spots/crown), hands off to cannon-es at the deck; pins = flat foot + 7 spheres (sphere contacts ~5x cheaper than cylinders), deck = Plane with contacts past its end / over gutters / below y -.025 dropped (narrowphase getContacts hook), SAPBroadphase + useBoundingBoxes (big win: sphere-vs-box tests on every static box), enableFrictionReduction (cannon caps friction per contact: many contacts ate the momentum), pins in the pit removed, fallen pins damped; TUNE {pp/pb/pd friction+restitution, drive .85->1.6 by entry angle (straight balls deflect, hooks carry), driveAng 5}; ~40 ms compute per roll. Pocket hook ~95% strikes, straight ball needs the exact pocket, edge hits leave 10/7/6-10/3-6-10, nose hits splits. bowl/rules.js (any length, last frame = 10th, foul = 0 + pins back, splits from the touching-pins graph + LEAVE_NAMES, streak names DOUBLE..HAMBONE..GOLDEN TURKEY..PERFECT), bowl/cpu.js (pocket / cross-lane spares, aim error by skill; skilled ~240, weak ~160), tests/bowl-sim.mjs (in test:core). bowl/gl.js own WebGL2 renderer (8 point lights + Blinn spec, emissive, fog, MIRROR pass under the lane = reflections), bowl/scene.js: 7 lanes, gutters, capping, kickbacks, masking per lane, ceiling fixtures, walls, ball returns, seats, overhead monitors (texture = the score canvas), sweep bar, decor (wall posters, rug, disco ball); every part painted per ERA (ocelot/starlanes/cosmic/reno/galactic) as canvas textures, drop-ins from src/data/bowlSprites.js (assets/bowling -> bowl:<name>, pwa LATER 'bowl:'). bowl/screens.js: score sheet + cartoon animations (strike, spare, split w/ name, pickup, gutter 'MUNSONED!', foul 'MARK IT ZERO!!', double, turkey, hambone, bagger, perfect, count/open) in the SCREENS era style (amber pixel CRT / cartoon TV / neon / slick HD / hologram), shown full screen + on the 3D monitor. src/data/bowlAlley.js = profile.bowlAlley (validAlley in profile.js): ERAS, PARTS room/lanes/masking/screens/lights/pins bought era by era (lanes change the oil), 11 BALLS (cover/revs/lb/rg), SHOES (steady/speed), DECOR, PERKS (aim-line, spare-ball, oil-man, tip-jar, replay), earnings per game. bowl/shop.js = Arcade alley screen (DOM over the 3D alley, live preview of an upgrade). Controls: drag ball = stance, drag lane = aim arrow (board at the arrows), swipe up = bowl (speed from the last 110 ms, v/(min(W,H)*7) = power, bend of the path = hook, power > 1.38 = foul), trail turns red. Cameras aim / chase / pin cam / shop drift (off-centre projection proj[9]) / intro fly-in. Online: seat 0 {setup}, {ball}, {aim} stream, {th} = release options (both phones roll it), bowler sends {fin} pins. s.debug: phase/cur/players/pins/anim/state()/swipe()/throw()/skip()/bot (assign true or call)/fast/shop{tab,act,go}. Templates tools/bowl-templates.mjs -> docs/bowling-sprites/templates (72), brief GPT-SPRITE-BRIEF.md, START-HERE row. tests/bowling.mjs (browser: shop, buy, real swipe, 1P vs CPU, 2P). Perks: spare-ball (house ball when pins are already down), replay (slow-motion re-run of a strike from the pin cam: same deterministic roll), oil-man (no lane breakdown; lanesPlayed adds wear otherwise), tip-jar, aim-line (setting). Distant pin crashes as alley ambience. NOT YET: heard by ears, real phone fps. SMB UNDERWATER SONG (v0.99.39): smbAudio SONGS water/'water-hurry' (loop null = whole file until measured) + hasSong; songFor plays it in areas with def.water (2-2) only when assets/music/smb-song-water.mp3 exists, else 'under'. The owner's rip is in Claude Share on the laptop (not reachable from cloud sessions): ask for it as a chat attachment or a GitHub upload, then measure its loop like the others.
ANGRY HEADS CAMERA (Nov 22, owner: tiny, sling off to the left, can't pull all the way, no zoom/scroll, slow dots after a shot): angryHeads.js cam.z zoom 0 (whole level) .. 1 (close, ~7.5 units tall) on a log scale (zoomRange), saved localStorage sc-ah-zoom (first time .7 on tall screens, .5 wide); pinch, +/- buttons bottom right (zoomBtns), wheel; drag empty space pans x AND y (cam.yb = world height at the ground line, GG = GY + yb*sc, GY = .86 H); aim camera aimX = sling - PULL - 2.2; pull is RELATIVE to where you grab (pullPx = clamp(PULL*sc,90,.3 min(W,H)) px = full pull), grab radius max(2.6 units, 64 px); flight camera follows the bird x/y then the moving bodies (actionX) once it stops; shot ends when the bird is still .5 s + fort quiet (quietFort: nothing faster than 1.1) or still 3.2 s or rolled 1.6 s, cap 9 s; a head on the ground loses speed fast (was rolling ~40 units past the fort = the slow dots); trail stops at the first hit; online snapshot cc (view centre) + cy. Checked: scratch ah.mjs (752x835/390x780/844x390: full pull, shot ends 3-5 s headless, zoom buttons), angry-sim, angry-heads.mjs both sizes.
ANGRY HEADS + FLAPPY DISASTER REBUILT, SKI RUN, PIPE/FIRE/POOL FIXES (Nov 21, RELEASED as v0.99.38 (owner: "Locked. Please release"; check-modules, smoke, unlocks, arcade-groups, angry-sim, smb-levels, fuzz flappy/angry/smb3/pool); owner: angry heads endless with location stages + location characters as pigs + items as specials, modern polished look; flappy endless getting harder with many random themed stages; 1P solo; online turns watched; pool watched aim choppy; a ski-run bonus in World 4; some side pipes / Bowser's fire faced the wrong way). FLAPPY = src/engines/flappyDisaster.js (engine flappy; old mobile.js flappy unused): VH 512 world, one life, course(seed) per pipe index (gap 196->136, spacing 262->196, speed by score 150->250, gap jumps grow, moving pipes from pipe 22 = gapY by screen position so the watcher matches), 16 STAGES (classic, meat locker, fuzzy wuzzy hell, Pokémon route, ninth circle, space, Davy Jones, Dunder Mifflap, graveyard, vaporwave, sugar rush, upside down, sewers, Lost Wages, brain freeze, disco) picked from the seed (localStorage sc-flappy-stage forces one for tests), medals 10/20/30/40, best in localStorage sc-flappy-best; solo kind 'game' (soloFinish, won = new best), 2P pass and play, NET_NATIVE: turns, runner streams {c,y,v,tr,sc,d,st} 20/s, watcher extrapolates, {done} event; tests/flappy.mjs (STAGES=all screenshots) + online-native. ANGRY HEADS = src/engines/angryHeads.js (engine headlaunch; old headlaunch.js unused) + angry/levels.js (buildLevel(n): episode = location (LPE 5, level 5 = boss castle with crowned king), pieces tower/pyramid/house/bridge/bunker/crates+TNT, hills from episode 3, stone/helmets/size grow with n, birds 3-6, item birds with abilities split/boost/bomb/egg/big from level 3, a bonus item hidden in the fort; stars thresholds) + angry/physics.js (cannon-es flat bodies: linearFactor z 0, angularFactor z only; forts start asleep, everything wakes on a break; damage by impact speed x mass ratio; TNT/bomb boom(); powers; settled()). EPISODES = LOCATIONS with characters that have raster art (location characters + same source) = 366 worlds; items = items of that source with art (+4 random). Map carousel (cards with location art ART['photo:location:<id>'] when added, else realm sky + icon + its heads) + 5 level bubbles with stars, unlocks by stars; profile.angryHeads {stars,best} validated in profile.js. Camera: intro pan fort->sling, follows the bird, drag to look around, sc = max(fit, W/34). 1P = solo kind 'levels' (result buttons map/retry/next/quit -> soloFinish), 2P = 3 levels each (board 2, level from the seed), NET_NATIVE: seat 0 picks {lv}, turn phone streams bodies/dead/birds/aim/camera/trail 15/s, watcher applies (no physics), {done:[cur,n,score,stars]}. Tests: tests/angry-sim.mjs (Node, in test:core: levels build, forts stand still, aiming bot clears early levels), tests/angry-heads.mjs (browser 1P + 2P), online-native angry-heads. SKI RUN: World 4 beanstalks (cfg.ice) lead to skiRun() instead of cloudArea (terraces down the mountain, kickers, moguls, a big kicker to a 1-up, lift kind 'rail' = static grind rails (riding = grinding: points, sparks, min speed 2), skis given on arrival, B in the air on skis = trick spin paid out on landing (TRICK x2)); debug.enter. FIXES: sideTile body tiles inset (mouth now the taller rim, pipes read the right way); Bowser's flame drawn with its head leading (scale by vx; ice flame too); paddysPool netAim 12/s + trailing send, watcher eases S.aimGoal (easeAim).
SMB REAL-GAME PIECES + STAGE STYLES (Nov 21, RELEASED in v0.99.38; owner: side of 1-2's Warp Zone pipes took you to the level end, a coin-room pipe left him stuck, stages all alike, wants every real-game element and real-game difficulty): FIXES marioBros side door needs p.x+HW within the pipe mouth (1-2's exit pipe at 142 caught anyone walking right in the Warp Zone on the same row); coinRoom/warpRoom ceilings start at column 4 (the drop at column 2 landed ON the ceiling). ENGINE: FOES bullet/hammerBro/podoboo/leaper (+ generic para (green, bounces; stomp -> koopa) and buzzy (14 px, shell like a Koopa)), FIREPROOF set (fireballs burn up on bullet/podoboo/buzzy/ghost); springAt (def.springs {c,r}: land = bounce -5.3, hold jump = -6.3 with gHold .125), def.cannons {c,r,h} fire pooled bullets at the runner (a.cn timers, not point blank), def.cheepZones [x0,x1] spawn pooled leapers from below, lifts 'fall' (drops while loaded) and 'bal' pairs (mate, ymax, rope; .75 px/f, rope snaps at ymax), loaded = onLift (own ride or geometry for the partner), def.loops {x,back,r0,r1} = castle maze gate (wrong feet row -> x-=back, camera snapped, WRONG WAY!; right row = chime). smbArt drawBuzzy/drawSpring/drawCannon + drawFoe bullet/hammerBro/podoboo, para wings. LEVELS: area helpers spring/cannon/cheeps/podoboo/balance/fallLift/loop; PIECES (cannons, hammers, springWall, bridge (BRIDGE planks at row 13 + cheeps), paras, balance, falls, loopLifts, buzzies, stairsPit, pipeField) + STYLE_PIECES; every Worlds 4-8 stage has a style (4-1 pipes, 4-2 buzzy, 4-3 balance, 4-4 maze, 5-1 classic, 5-2 fortress, 5-3 bullet, 5-4 lava, 6-1 bridge, 6-2 lifts, 6-3 falls, 6-4 gauntlet, 7-1 hammer, 7-2 pipemaze, 7-3 cheep, 7-4 maze, 8-1 fortress, 8-2 bullet, 8-3 bridge, 8-4 final): >=3 style pieces + ~45% picks; four genOver openings, two genUnder openings, flats always carry blocks/steps/pipes and often end in a pit, W 225; genAthletic styles balance/falls/bullet/cheep; genCastle = CASTLE_PLANS of room/maze/lava/gaunt segments (podoboos from World 4, hammer bro before the bridge from World 6, final 8-4 = 5 segments, time 400). 2-1 and 3-1 rebuilt (no longer 1-1's opening): spring wall, Bill Blasters, Paratroopas; 3-1 plank bridges with leaping fish + spring crate stack; 2-4/3-4 lava pits got podoboos. Tests: tests/mario-pieces.mjs (browser: both pipe fixes + every piece working + census of pieces per stage), smb-levels.mjs drop-in check (catches the ceiling bug), mario.mjs + mario-secrets + mario-board + fuzz pass. Run browser tests one at a time (three at once starve rAF: everything reads 0). Scratch map renderer: scratchpad gen/dump.mjs + draw.py.

SMB SECRETS + SKI RAMPS + WORLD SELECT (Nov 21, RELEASED in v0.99.37; owner after playing 4-2: levels too simple, a cloud-rider stage, more enterable pipes, beanstalks to cloud areas, more 1-ups, steady difficulty, 4-2 secret to Worlds 5/6/7, climbers throw hammers, penguins go off ledges, world select for beaten Bowsers, more ski ramps + alternate paths, no arrows over enterable pipes). smbLevels.js: difficulty lv = world + (stage-1)/4 drives gaps/lifts/piranhas; evenFoes() tops up/trims walkers to 4+2.1*lv (caves 3+1.9*lv); new outdoor sections tiers / floating islands / pipe hops / brick bridge; cave sections pipes+plants / brick shelf / brick stairs; `pending()` forces a coin-room pipe (to area 'bonus' = coinRoom(kind), out via a later pipe exitOf), the beanstalk block (content 'vine') and its landing stretch; cloudArea() = kind 'sky' (cloud tiles) with coins + 1-up, def.fallTo drops you back into main at the landing column; World 4 ramps: piece variants gap jump / HIGH ROUTE (big kicker {big:true} → ice ledge row 7 with coins + 1-up and its own ramp {r:7}) / moguls, small ramps at the end of long flats, ice-cave ramps, ramps on 4-3 building tops; 4-2 = warp shelf (hidden coin block at row 10 → brick shelf row 7 → pipe to area 'warp' = warpRoom pipes 5/6/7); 5-1 rider:true (foe 'rider' + 6 pooled 'spiny' slots {pool:true}, def.riderEnd); 2-1 and 3-1 also got a coin-room pipe + beanstalk. marioBros.js: vines per area (growVine on bump, {vn} online, drawn by smbArt drawVine), p.climb (hold jump climbs, down climbs down, left/right lets go; top of the screen → def.vineTo; pose 'pole'; PF flag 'climb'), FOES rider (follows above you, throws spinies from free pool slots, respawns 7 s after a stomp until riderEnd) + spiny (egg → walk, NO_STOMP), climber hammers (w.flames look 'hammer' with gravity f.g), penguins no ledge turn, ramps have row r + big (x1.25 launch), BIG AIR / HUGE AIR points on landing (p.airT), ▼ pipe hint removed, enemy speed per stage. Stage list = 32 (stage index = (world-1)*4 + n-1). World 3 gained slasher/dreamer/imp. marioBros.js: FOES table (vx, start state, hp, height) + behaviours: slasher (stomp = knocked down 3 s), dreamer (fades, reappears behind you), imp/dice hop, climber/penguin turn at ledges, icicle (shake then drop), card wave, ptero dive, raptor/brute charge (raptor leaps walls), dilo/floater/angel/cherub shoot w.flames looks venom/fire/bolt/arrow, skull dashes, trex 6 hp and unstompable; NO_STOMP cactus/icicle/swimmers. Power-ups from blocks: skis (World 4: def.ice = slippery, skis fast 3.5 + ski-jump ramps def.ramps with a spin), cape (World 5: full-run jump = 150-frame flight, hold jump = glide), mega (World 6: MEGA_T 600, pH 70, megaSmash breaks bricks/blocks/pipes, kills on touch), yoshi (World 7: ride +12 px, B = tongue eats, hold jump = flutter, a hit = Yoshi runs off); hurt order yoshi > cape > skis > size. Wind (5-2) windOn() 3.5 s of every 8 pushes .45/.6 px/f. Difficulty: makeWorld k = 1+.07*(world-1) enemy speed, fbK firebars, Bowser hp 5+(world-1) (God 14) and timers /(1+.12*(world-1)). Bowsers: ski (beanie, goggles, frost breath), elvis (jumpsuit, pompadour, shades, note breath), safari (pith helmet), devil (red, horns, wings, pitchfork), god (glow, halo, beard, holy beams). Finale: axe phase 'ascend' FINALE_T 960 frames (golden glow + halo + 'X HAS BECOME A GOD!', then EVIL_PAL + horns + angry face 'BUT ABSOLUTE POWER... CORRUPTS ABSOLUTELY.', black 'TO BE CONTINUED...') then stageClear. Warp pipes 2/3/4 → worlds 2/3/4. smbArt: tile sets + drawThemeBg/drawThemeProp per kind, drawFoe, items skis/cape/mega/yoshi egg, flame looks. tests/mario.mjs runs 3-4 → 4-1 → … → 8-4 finale with skis/cape/wind/mega/yoshi checks.

BOULDER RUN GETAWAY (Nov 20, RELEASED as v0.99.31 (owner: "Release"; fuzz, solo, online-native raiders, smoke); owner: see the old chaser fall and get away, think you're safe, then the next one comes from somewhere and you run again): sim.js BRK timeline (s since the zone corner): slow 1.6 / stop 2.6 / look 2.8 / back 4.0 / enter 5.0 / land 5.8 / go 6.3 / end 7.3 = CALM; calmSpeed(e) (run, ease to a stop, still, sprint ramp), R.brkT(); new chaser lands min(BRK_GAP 10, a-1.5) behind (on the new straight, never round the corner); no input/tilt during it. track.js gate straights +44 m, obstacles from 56 m. templeRun.js: old chaser misses the corner (missAt: trail to the straight's end, then straight on over the far wall, falls .5g(t)^2; Hovitos/Thuggee leader goes over, the other two stop at the edge 'yes'); ESCAPE banner + crash when it goes over, boom 1.1 s later; camera keyframes P1 front looking back / P2 3/4 front at the look-back / P3 face close-up / P4 pulled back for the entrance / back behind, blended by orbiting round the runner (tight mid-swing so it stays over the path); getawayActor = jog, idle, turn 0.88 pi to look back (think face), turn back (win face + phew), flinch 'stumble' + sad face + glance at the landing, run; music held from stop to enter (W.quiet, also honoured by pause); ENTRY per chaser: drop from above (boulder, cart, Hovitos), rise out of the floor (rats, cobra), climb up over the side (Thuggee, T-Rex), zone title at land. world.js drawRats/drawCobra take at()[3] = height offset; gate signs have a mirrored back face. Watching online: snapshot field b = their R.brkT() (mirror.brkT = b + snapshot age; old builds fall back to the local time of their zone change), mirror speed x calmSpeed; checked in step within .2 s. debug nearZone(z) (just before the corner into zone z), brk. Checked: scratch gw.mjs frame sheets 390x780 + 844x390 for every chaser kind, games.mjs fuzz, solo.mjs raiders, sim bots (perfect 400 s).

BOULDER RUN = TEMPLE RUN 3D (Nov 19-20, RELEASED as v0.99.26 (owner: "Yes release"; smoke, pwa-lazy, raiders fuzz, solo); power-ups magnet/boost/shield (sim.js POW, track.js pows) + trun/music.js in; v0.99.27 = screen left/right swapped in templeRun.js input + turn arrows (camera looking +Z puts the course's +X 'right' on the screen's left; sim/course L/R unchanged); owner: look and play just like the real Temple
Run in 3D, guy = Indiana Jones body, girl = Tomb Raider body; chasers change per stage, stage 1 = the boulder; 2P take turns,
1P endless high score; 3D models, no sprites). STEP 1 (runners): Quaternius CC0 packs (Universal Base Characters, Modular Outfits
Fantasy, Universal Animation Library 1+2; one 65-joint skeleton) in assets/originals/trun; tools/trun/build-chars.py ->
assets/trun/{indy,lara,anims}.skm.gz + <char>-<skin|cloth|hair|eyes>.webp (Indy = Superhero_Male + Ranger clothes recoloured,
skin under clothes culled; Lara = Superhero_Female + Ranger boots, tank/shorts/gloves painted on the skin texture). Cloud env
'ShambConkins' (personal) allows itch.io + its R2 host (itchio-mirror.cb031a832f44726753d6267436f3b414.r2.cloudflarestorage.com),
quaternius.com, kenney.nl, opengameart.org, drive hosts (itch free flow: POST /download_url with the page csrf, then POST
/<slug>/file/<upload id>?source=game_download -> signed url). STEP 2 (playable, Nov 20): engine ballrun2p = src/engines/templeRun.js
(old 2D boulderRun.js unused). trun/track.js makeCourse(seed): straights with L/R/T corners built ahead (content from seed+straight
number only, so 2P meet the same things; T arms previewed via world peek), obstacles root (jump; touch = stumble)/log/arch/fire/
gap/block(lanes) + coin lines/arcs, spacing grows with speed (perfect bot survives 400 s). trun/sim.js makeRunner (lanes LANE
1.05, jump 10.4/g 30, slide .78 s, turn window 8 m, corner snap at the corner centre, run off = 'edge', 2 stumbles within 7.5 s =
'caught', chase CHASE 14 / CLOSE 2.2) + makeBot(skill) + simulate() (CPU opponent: skill .8+.16*cpuSkill on seed+':run'; skill .9
~430 m median). trun/world.js: per-straight meshes in local space (stone deck + cliff wall into height fog, parapets, corner
walls, temple facade + tunnel at straight 0, obstacles, trees/pillars/walls), canvas-painted textures, coins (spin), fire
billboards, boulder (rolls on the runner's trail, camera lifts over it when close). render.js has height fog (hfog) + rim scaled
by albedo. trun/music.js = original drum groove (levels by speed, Music setting). Face = decal patch on the Head bone (faceProp:
headFacts.drawFace into a 256 canvas, ellipsoid patch ax 2.9), model eyes hidden. Intro 2.6 s: camera in front at the temple door
(boulder rolls out), swings behind, RUN!. 2P = s.pass per runner (summary of P1 on P2's card); online = both run (netField hz 6
{d,sc,al}, partner metres in the HUD, finish swap). s.debug: runner, course, phase, autopilot(), speed(k), manual()+tick(dt), kill(),
faces, bot (online). build.mjs assets/trun -> trun:<name>, pwa LATER 'trun:'. Checked: Node sim bot table, games.mjs fuzz, tests/
solo.mjs raiders, online-native raiders (wrangler) OK, scratch tr/play.mjs (+P2=1 second runner, FACE=1 dumps the face texture),
tr/clip.mjs (frame-by-frame video). Headless 3D is software (~3 fps): use speed()/manual ticks in tests.

star backdrop canvas (third full-screen layer redrawn every frame). Fixes: render.js canvas() = willReadFrequently (every helper

hole's gradient/clouds drawn by the GL layer (drawStarsGL/drawSkyGL, glLayer alpha(a)); bg canvas no longer used. SPEED TEST +2
rows paintgpu/paintcpu (4 painted squares + copy, chip vs memory canvas) to confirm on the phone; results screen rows fit 844x390.

MINIGOLF GPU LAYER (Nov 17, RELEASED as v0.99.17 (owner: "Release"; minigolf tests + smoke); owner's meter screenshots:

0.4 ms work but 517 ms gaps, a first run at 4 fps): drawing big 2D canvases into the screen canvas costs per frame on his phone (the
browser re-sends them to the GPU). NEW src/engines/minigolf/glLayer.js = WebGL2 canvas UNDER the 2D game canvas (main canvas made
position:relative z 1; GL absolute z 0, same box via ResizeObserver; bg 2D canvas under it for sky/space backdrops): textures (texStorage,
premultiplied uploads), batched quads, repeat pattern (lawn), dyn/dynRect per-frame uploads (UNPACK_SKIP sub-rects), tick() hides it on
frames that don't draw, lost -> puttPutt drops to 2D (caches rebuild: G.gl/SP.gl mismatch). render.js setGL(): ground tiles painted in a
258 px scratch canvas then texSubImage2D into page textures (no 2D page canvases), low copy -> texture, lawn -> GL pattern, anim runs
(water ripples; pads/conveyors/lost ball + the trail as 'tail') -> screen scratch, only the drawn box uploaded (x._bbx via onCanvas/flat/
conveyor/trail), static sprites -> atlas textures (painted in a scratch), drawn as GL quads in depth order; live items (balls, movers,
bumpers, magnets, flag, props drawn live) on the 2D canvas, each with a screen box (BB/propBB/ballBB/ptsBB/millBB/clownBB); a static
sprite after a live item that overlaps it ERASES its shape from the 2D canvas (destination-out from a lazily painted own canvas, drawOcc)

MINIGOLF HOLE-START + DIPS (Nov 17, RELEASED as v0.99.15 (owner: "Release"; test:core EXIT 0); owner: chugs at the start of holes,
dips under 60; don't change the look): render.js prepareHole(C,course,rot,kc,until) paints a hole's caches ahead (slope shading per
green, lawn square, low copy one run per call, big page canvases one per call, ground squares cup-first, every sprite); puttPutt
prepStep(i,ms): hole 1 on the loading screen (bar 30-94%, max 6 s), each next hole while the scorecard is up (cardT>.3, 3 ms on
120 Hz / 6 ms); P.compile keeps one C per hole so startHole just uses it (prepared C freed on exit/skip). Hole 1's first frame is
drawn once under the loading screen (`warm`); ball faces for every size step x 4 moods made while loading. pic()/MISS in render.js:
a cache repaints only when a picture IT was painted without arrives (any of the ~60 pictures loading used to repaint everything);
lawn squares cached per course+turn (LAWNS). cupField: cell z cached + 4-unit wall buckets, identical distances (checked all 18), ~2x
faster, computed in prep (was 3-50 ms mid-hole). saveProfile on sink/lost ball deferred to the scorecard/round end/exit (saveSoon;
it re-reads the whole save: ~30 ms at 4x on every sink). Pacing: fallback to 60 retries full speed only while a person aims, hold
doubles after a failed try (4 s .. 60 s); CPU thinks 3 ms/frame at full 120, else 7 (was 9). pace.win no longer grows unbounded.
Measured headless 4x CPU with no-op drawing (scratch count.mjs): flyover frames now paint nothing (were 100-136 ms first frames),
longest frames ~60 ms on the static scorecard. Old vs new screenshots, all 18 holes, aim + whole hole, 390x780 + 844x390, with a
frame-locked clock (scratch shots.mjs): pixel-identical except the download toast. minigolf.mjs both sizes, minigolf-sim, golf fuzz,
online-native happy-gilmore-golf (+LAG) pass. Not on a real phone. Idea if CPU turns still dip: run cpu.js plan in a Worker.

fixed zoom st.kc (= puttPutt kCeil(), the closest the auto camera gets; capped by GROUND_PX 18 Mpx): 256 px ground tiles
painted within st.paintMs per frame (intro 7, aim 5, roll 2 ms; visible first, then the whole extent) + one small `low`
copy of the hole (LOW_K .4) that stands in for unpainted tiles and is used when zoomed far out; sprites at the same kc
(unpainted ones drawn live); a picture loading later marks things stale (drawn as before until repainted). Lawn = live
pattern from one prescaled square (drawLawn). Pictures: mip chains (mipChain/shrunk, halvings, picked by size at kc via
KREF), made on the loading screen (warmPictures); minigolfSprites decode() before a picture counts as loaded,
readyMinigolf/coursePictures/MENU_PICTURES. puttPutt: phase 'loading' (bar) waits for the picker's pictures (10 s cap)
and, after TEE OFF, the course's (12 s) + mips; picker drawn once into menuCv (was the 4K title-bg scaled every frame);
ball face sprites in 2^(1/6) size steps; freeHole(C) on hole change/exit; debug.gfx() = cacheStats (tiles/todo/sprites).

done by the end of the flyover on gilmore 1-3. Headless frame intervals are software-raster bound (not phone-like).

2 sizes with a real drag, 2P hand-off), online-native words-n-shit ± LAG, games.mjs fuzz. SQWORDLE HINT: sqwordleRules HINTS 2 /

MINIGOLF FRAME RATE (Nov 16, RELEASED as v0.99.10; owner: too low a framerate to play): render.js used to repaint the whole
course every frame (lawn/green patterns, clipped rail faces with gradients, shading, big pictures) = ~18-30 fps at 4x CPU. The
view is affine (camera = pan + zoom), so now: groundRuns = lawn/shadows/greens/still ground pieces painted into 1-2 screen-aligned
layers round the view (margin .3 x screen, LAYER_PX 4.5M budget, world origin on a whole layer pixel), anim steps (water ripples,
the void swirl, boost/conveyor/portal/lost ball) drawn live between them; standingSprites = every rail piece, block and scenery
prop painted once into its own sprite, sorted per frame with the live things (movers, bumpers, magnets, flag, balls) by depth().
Repaint: view leaves the layer, zoom 12% in (then 1.3x sharper) or 2x out, camera still for 8 frames at another zoom (exact 1:1
copies, whole pixels), art version change (minigolfSprites version()), hole turn. shrunk(img,sc) = pictures drawn small (windmill,
clown, gator, gopher, bumpers, balls, props) shrunk once in 1.25x steps. Also fixed: texture() kept a drawn stand-in forever if
the picture loaded after first use (the turf/rail/space art often never showed). 4x CPU headless: plain holes 26 -> 50-58 fps,
windmill/gator/spinner holes 17-24 -> 30-45 (rest = big live pictures + scaled layer copies, cheap on a phone GPU). Checked:
cached vs fully repainted frames match on all 18 holes at 390x780 and 844x390 (only moving things differ). Not on a real phone.
Round 2 (owner: still slightly low): the ground is 256 px TILES in layer space (groundTiles/prepareTiles/drawGround in
render.js): painted 2 a frame ahead of the camera (4 when still), a zoom starts a new set while the old one fills holes
(clipped, never drawn twice), a still camera gets a set at its exact zoom; a run that drew nothing in a tile keeps no canvas
(x._drew from drawGreen/flat/lawn; water holes' upper run is mostly empty); onCanvas() culls greens per tile. puttPutt.js: HUD
plate + chips cached (signature), camera snaps when within .4% / .6 px, stars painted once and slid (drawStars). Headless fps
is too noisy to tune further (software raster: one full-screen copy 3.6 ms at 4x, scaled 7.3 ms): measure ms/frame with CDP
TaskDuration instead (scratch perf2.mjs). Not on a real phone.

the guns, funny walk, bodies float, fire barrels still drawn, minigunners too big, every gun must dig): bro-body `hands` = the fist
measured per frame (was a fixed hand 8 px behind the fist; the fist rises 10 px running) → handAt(d,p) via p.bodyRow/bodyFr;

shots from FOE_MUZZLE spots (mook/bruiser/robot/mechtank/cultist/chariot/plumber/cannon); STRIDE run 58 / walk 40 world px per cycle;
CORPSE_SINK 2.5; bruiser 1.75; holy/fireball/boomerang/lightning dig (C.digTile; zap bites the first block in range; flame stays
slowest). NEW SLOTS (owner picked): bro-torso (armless, idle/walk 8/run 8/jump/fall/climb/ladder-with-arms, shoulder [26,37]) +
arms-<15 guns> (160x80, hold 1 + fire 4, shoulder [32,28], grip [62,42], muzzle per gun; fireFrame: cycle per shot, fast guns
loop); an arms sheet replaces bro-body + gun picture only for that gun. Props: prop-brazier/banner sheets, prop-tower/lamp/post/wire/

HEAD CHECK (Nov 11; round 2 Nov 11 late, released in v0.96.0): tools/head-check.py measures
every character picture (2,474) → src/data/headFacts.js HEAD_FACTS[key]=[face box, head box, look, conf, found, aspect].
Round 2 (owner: face size jumped between moods, faces looked the wrong way): YuNet landmarks (tools/models/
face_detection_yunet_2023mar.onnx, MIT; `pip install "opencv-python-headless<5"`) are the main detector: face width =
2.39 x eye distance (turn-corrected, mixed with eye-to-mouth on strong turns; 2.39 = old median, so faces kept their
average size), centre .48 S below the eyes, look = nose vs eye/mouth middle (|yaw| ≥ .08). even() per character: moods
without landmarks take the character's face/head ratio, sure landmark faces clamp to ±30% of it (helmets). Haar/outline =
fallback only (found 2,199). Was: Sean's two pictures measured .99 and .56 of the picture. src/headFacts.js
drawFace(s,g,i,cx,cy,size,mood,dir,faceK=.6) = face on the spot + mirror against the body's facing; used by Seanic
(sonic3.js head) and SotN (sotn.js headFn, sotnArt passes facing). Check sheets: local-test-reports/head-check.
Not wired yet: Broforce/Sundown/baseball/DOOM/bosses. Sean's happy/walk/mad/horror picture is a car selfie with the
background (no cut-out): shows as a rectangle on bodies.

ONLINE TURN SPEED (Nov 5, owner: turn games (esp. Mancala) sometimes show the other player's move late though they already made it): netPlay confirmations every ≤150 ms (was 900); a move unconfirmed for 2.5 s is resent and net.js probeNet() pings: no reply in 2.5 s = half-open socket → reconnectNet (was only the 35 s silence check); sendNet on a dead socket kick()s an immediate reconnect (no backoff wait). netTurns (classicBoards) sets net.held while their move waits for this phone; proMancala behind() (= held or ready) ends cutaways after .2 s, flies gems 2.5x and shortens combo pauses so the watcher catches up. online-native mancala/four-lights/reversi/checkers/uno/dots/pong/air hockey pass (± LAG).
CHAT (I4, Nov 5, owner: canned + typed, everywhere online + friends any time, bubble with face, huge pool with normal and super crass lines, a few positive and negative dealt fresh each time): src/chat.js + src/data/chatLines.js (NICE/TRASH/CRASS/SITUATION myturn|theirturn|game|room/EMOTES). Room: sendNet {k:'chat',id,text,name,character} (all other listeners ignore it). Friends: friends.js sendFriendChat/onFriendChat/friendOf/friendMe, Lobby relays {t:'chat',to,id,text} → {t:'chat',id,text,from} + {t:'chat-sent',ok} (worker.js; live after the next Worker deploy), 💬 per online friend row → duel.js openChat(code). UI: #sc-chat fab (right edge 44%, unread count; reparented INTO #dialog[open], since a modal makes the rest inert and popovers over a modal are inert too), #sc-chat-dlg = its own modal <dialog> bottom sheet (deal(): 2 now + 3 nice + 4 nasty (2 CRASS unless profile.settings.chatClean) + character quote, avoids the last 60 dealt; 🎲 re-deal; typing; last 6 messages), #sc-chat-bubbles popover (shows over dialogs, 4.6 s, tap = reply), window capture key listeners stop keys reaching games while the sheet is open. Settings row Clean chat (chatClean whitelisted). tests/chat.mjs (wrangler + served --pwa, 18 checks); friends/online/online-board still pass.
BASEBALL GRIFFEY LOOK (Nov 5, after v0.86.0, owner's 2 Griffey SNES clips + "ball ends before the batter / batter not in the box / should look exactly like the original"): griffeyArt batLayout = STAGE of the Griffey picture (ASPECT 1.376, u,v 0..1, Sw=W/.8 or fit height; tall phones see outfield/wall/stands above it): mound (.5,.2), infield grass ellipse (.5,.2,.76,.41), dirt arc + 2nd base above, foul wedges/lines, boxes [.233-.40]/[.60-.767] at v .735, plate (.5,.84) pw .046Sw; T=.43Sh batter height, zone at feet - .56T (bat height at contact), zone.w = plate width. batter(): feet placed so the sheet's `sweet` point (contact frame) crosses zoneXY(sweetU) = where the swing is judged; face + helmet drawn BEHIND the sheet (bat in front). Pitch: perspective (PR 3) from the hand to the zone point, then into a catcher's mitt (A.mitt rises from below the screen to the pitch, squeezes; no catcher/umpire drawn = catcher's view like the game), MPH after the pitch. HUD = A.hudTop/hudCount/hudBatter/hudBases (griffeyBall hud()), touch hints at the bottom, BUNT above the diamond. Innings menu = A.standee (batter sheet). Batter sheets repainted by tools/baseball/long-bat.py from assets/originals/baseball (bat 64 px, frames padded to 168x172, def foot [72,171] head [74,48] sweet [138,85]); brief/README/templates updated (catcher/umpire sheets not needed now). drawField uses the same GRASS/CLAY palette. A GPT redraw of the batter seen from behind (like the game) would get closer still. ARCADE SWIPE SMOOTH (M25, Nov 5, after v0.85.0; owner: 'swiping between games is really choppy', video on Drive too big for the connector (10.7 MB > 10 MB), reproduced instead): at release the page froze 100-350 ms at 4x CPU (drawFill drew the full 1600 px <img> into the 32 px fill canvas = synchronous main-thread decode; focusTile did everything at once) and the text jumped without sliding out. Now (arcadeConsole.js): fill = fetch → createImageBitmap(resize 32) off the main thread, cached per URL (`fills`, also warmed with the ±1 neighbours); `prepSwipe` (one frame after the drag starts) puts the next game's picture in the hidden key slot (decoded, `placeKey(img,false)` = no shade change; `applyShade` later) and builds its text off-page (`buildInfo`); the drag moves the FRONT slot (`translate`, fades to ≥.78) over the prepared one, which is fully shown UNDERNEATH (z-index: .arc-key-front 1, .arc-key-leaving 2 while a swiped-away slot fades), so the gap reveals the next picture (owner's 2nd clip of v0.85.0, Fold 1200x1080 recording: a solid wash-coloured block showed in the gap, the text vanished for 2-3 frames, then picture and text popped in separately); release = `swapKeyArt(...,{pre,dir,W})` (old slot drifts on + fades, `keyOut`) + `swipeInfo` (Web Animations: old text out at finger speed from the drag's dx, new text in from the other side; no style read-back); tile highlight/row scroll/record chip/warming + the --arc-h wash and the text shade wait 420 ms (`rest()` in focusTile; each is a full-screen repaint). No page-wide `arc-swiping` class (first move restyled everything): user-select:none always on .arcade-console, will-change inline on info/hero during a drag; width cached at pointerdown. Tests: tests/arcade-swipe-smooth.mjs (frame gaps + text motion + next picture under the finger; VIEW=; 0 frames >50 ms at 390x780/752x835/844x390/1100x700, v0.85.0 fails it), arcade-layers.mjs SWIPE=1 (13 screens, same as taps), arcade-swipe/console/perf pass. Not yet seen on the owner's phone.

ARCADE FLICKER (Nov 5, owner's Fold screen recording: whole screen blank 2-3 frames when tapping/swiping fast; frames + YAVG dips via ffmpeg signalstats): GPU memory exhaustion from stacked layers, not the fades. arcadeConsole.js: swapKeyArt = exactly two `.arc-keyart` slots (`.arc-key-front` opacity 1, other 0, .22 s opacity transition in theme.css end), new picture into the hidden slot, decode, placeKey, swap, old slot emptied after 260 ms, a tap mid-fade finishes it; keyfill = 32 px canvas (`drawFill`, darkened) instead of CSS blur(26px) on a 112% copy; no arc-kenburns; hero art swapLayer = decode first, opacity-only `.arc-fade-in/out`, old removed; text = `swapInfo` (built off DOM, imgs decoded ≤300 ms, replaced in one frame, no arc-info-in fade from 0; swipe = transform glide only); --arc-h transition removed; warm decode only ±1. Tests: tests/arcade-perf.mjs (CDP trace, CPU 4x: style 351→54 ms/move, raster 292→145, BREAKDOWN=1 per element), tests/arcade-layers.mjs (LayerTree peak while tapping every GAP ms: old 21-24 screens growing with speed, new ~13.6 flat; fails over MAX_SCREENS, default 16), screencast brightness check (no blank frames). BASEBALL GRIFFEY CONTROLS (Nov 5, after v0.84.0, owner: "gameplay totally sucks... batter should move around the batter's box like the real game, the bat looks ridiculous"): griffeyBall st.bx {x,y,plane mid|hi|lo,bunt} (x = toward the plate, sweetU = -.2+1.05x; y = up in the box: meets the ball .035 s earlier and before .17 of the break), touches by roleAt (both local: top 45% pitcher, bottom batter; else the whole screen): batter left half drag = move, right half = swing on touch-down (SWING_LAG .1 s to contact), flick up/down sets plane until contact, BUNT button (judge: contact when |du|<.6, slow roller); pitcher drag = st.rubber before (plan target u = rubber*.55), pick pitch = throw at once, drag in flight = aftertouch st.pitch.steer capped per kind (STEER), locks at LOCK .72 (lockLater sends {sl:[pn,u,v]}), streamed {sa}; batter phone waits for sl before judge; batter box streamed {bx}. CPU batter reads finalPlan at flight .55 (batterWindow). Rules retuned (cpuPitch edges, cpuSwing patience/timing, foul q<.46, wider spray): sim per pitch take 43 / miss 11 / foul 16 / in play 30 / HR 1.4%. New bat (griffeyArt drawBat, foreshortened mid-swing). tests/baseball.mjs drives rubber/pitch/steer/move/swing.

BASEBALL D4 (Nov 4, same branch; owner: innings 1/3/9 Arcade, board 1; roster + Major League cast; Bat & Field = real game, Batting Cage = Derby): engine keys baseballfield + baseball → engines/griffeyBall.js (mode from engine/id; old baseballField.js/homeRunDerby.js unused). Rules/physics engines/baseballRules.js (feet/secs, home (0,0), y to CF; BASES, POSITIONS P C 1B 2B SS 3B LF CF RF, fenceAt 330+70cos2θ, PITCHES + PITCH_SLOW 2.35, pitchPlan/pitchAt (late break), contact(plan,dt,cu,cv,bat) → miss/foul/fair {mph,phi,theta} (WINDOW .14, early dt<0 = pull = theta<0 = left field, q<.32 foul tip, bat.boost for the Derby), flight(hit) = 60 Hz samples with drag/bounces/roll/fence (hr, wall, landed, foul), ballAt, intercept (reaction .45 s, air flag), throwTime, tally/walk, ratings(id,over), cpuPitch/cpuSwing (breaking balls fool it, change-ups get it early)). Art engines/griffeyArt.js (battingBG cached, player/batter/catcherUmp, fieldCam/drawField/ballShadow, scoreboard, ticker, button, bigText). Flow: plateAppearance → onePitch (pitcher: local st.ui mode 'pitch' → pick + tap zone | cpu | remote recv('pt')) → swing phase (local: hold = aim with the cursor 80 px above the finger, release = doSwing; cpu: scheduled; remote recv('sw')) → judge; 'hit' → runPlay: makePlay(f,auth) sim on the fielding side (chaser by intercept, covers, catch/pickup, auto-throw after 2.2 s for a human or bestThrow for CPU, throwReq from base buttons, runners with AI extra bases and GO/BACK st.runCmd, force outs on throws or stepping on the base, third-out force wipes runs, 20 s cap) → summary {outs,runs,bases,kind,text}; online the fielding phone streams {fs} 15 Hz and sends {pl}, batting phone views (viewState) and sends {rn}. Innings loop with extras to innings+2, walk-off, no bottom half when home leads. Derby: 10 outs each vs BP pitches (online: each phone its own, {dfin}). data/majorLeague.js: 9 ML (1989) characters (ml-*), ML_RATINGS, DOYLE lines, TEAM_NAMES; contentPolicy adds them. solo kinds both 'cpu', both in NET_NATIVE. st.log/st.plog = play/pitch logs (tests). Tests: tests/baseball.mjs (rules + real taps + bots at 3 sizes + vs CPU + Derby). Bot-game spread ~29 outs / 11 hits per 40 balls in play. Not on a real phone; sounds not heard.
CASINO ROUND 2 (Nov 4, same branch; owner approved the blackjack screenshots, then: "continue with the rest of the casino games"): engine keys baccarat/poker/roulette → engines/vegasBaccarat.js, vegasPoker.js, vegasRoulette.js (old casinoBaccarat/casinoPoker/casinoRoulette/baccarat.js unused; casinoTable.js still used by Uno's casinoTap). Shared scaffold engines/vegasGame.js `createVegas(s,{seatsUsed,maxRegulars,themeId})` = canvas+fullBleed, theme/dealer/regulars/seats (players seats 1/3), `ask(p,mode,opts,fallback)` (local st.ui / CPU opts.cpu() / online net.next(); local answers net.send), W8/STOP, banners (fit to width), dealer/seat speech+moods, panelFrame helpers (text/small/smallWrap/buttons/chips/wait), drawSeat, flights, hud, input (hits → G.act, else onDown/onDrag/onUp/onTap/onSwipe, G.key), bots (st.auto / s.debug.bot → G.bot(u); generic 'bet' leave at st.leaveAt unless opts.noLeave), G.end (dealer unlock + finish), G.run. Pure rules engines/casinoRules.js (bacTotal/bankerDraws, pokerRank/compareRank/handName/drawPlan/handStrength, WHEEL/RED/betReturn/betLabel). Baccarat: Bond theme fixed, 8-deck shoe, 10 coups, PLAYER/BANKER boxes (L.box), squeeze mode (drag up = peel, {sq}), bead road; msgs {bet,side}|{leave},{sq}. Poker: ANTE 10, limits 20/40, CAP 4, HANDS 8, button rotates, regulars' `think` on G.rand (shared), side pots by contribution levels, rail = big hand of the chooser (G.shared() → face down until SHOW, st.peek), msgs {in}|{leave},{a:f|c|r},{d:[idx]}. Roulette: own layout (portrait: wheel top, vertical layout 3 cols + dozens + outside; wide: wheel left, horizontal layout), cached felt/wheel, ball path in wheelState (b0→bEnd laps, drop .55-.8, rides the pocket), player chip colours OWN, msgs {bets:[[key,amt]]}|{leave}, 10 spins, MIN 10 MAX 500. Kit: button tone 'blue', sounds whir/clack. Tests: tests/casino.mjs (rules, real taps per table, all 3 to the result at 3 sizes, vs CPU + dealer unlock), games.mjs fuzz 4/4 clean, online-native all four with and without LAG=80-150. Not on a real phone.

SOAK Nov 4 (released in v0.83.3; v0.83.0 overnight, 14 fails: solo-board 9, vault 4, online-board 1) = ONE cause: Pokémon battles on autopilot hung when the move the autopilot picks had no PP ("No PP left" → menu → same move, forever). v0.80.7 (Nov 3, owner asked to push to main): PICTURE SPEED. sw.js `file()` looked up every a/ file with `ignoreSearch:true`, which reads every cache entry: 575 ms per picture with a disk-backed cache of 2,600 files vs 0.6 ms exact (measure with a persistent context on a SHORT profile path, an in-memory context hides it, a long Windows path gives ERR_FAILED). Exact match now, SW_VERSION 5. Never use ignoreSearch on sc-assets. Arcade: `warmKeyArt` holds every key art fetched (near the focus decoded), `swapKeyArt` keeps the old picture until the new one is decoded and fades it in on top; has-key + hero art change at that moment. At 4x CPU, tile tap to background shown was 900-1650 ms, now 230-560. warmFaces keeps its images and also runs from requestGame; pass card imgs decoding=sync. v0.80.2 (Nov 3, owner asked to release, UI only, no suite run): Arcade key art FILLS the screen. KEY_BOX = tight boxes round Sean's + Amber's faces (found with OpenCV Haar + checked by eye; Ducks kept unflipped, faces only, owner's pick). placeKey: wide screens pick the bigger of 'right of the text' / 'full width above the text'; scale = min(cover, fit x SPILL 1.3, box ≤ 94% of the screen) so faces may spill a little into the text edge (bias: tall = keep top, wide = keep right) rather than zoom out; `.key-above` skips the side shade; `.arc-keyfill` = blurred cover copy shown only when the picture doesn't reach an edge. Checked by screenshots at 344x882, 390x780, 690x830, 830x690, 844x390, 900x1000, 1000x900.

v0.76.0 (Nov 3, released at the owner's request): the Nov 2 night work below + Arcade unlocks back ON (ARCADE_RECRUITS=true).
v0.75.0 (Nov 2, owner asked to release it): the boss-fight overhaul below. Fresh sessions: `docs/HANDOFF.md` first.
Owner's master to-do list is `docs/ROADMAP.md` (phases; "continue" = offer options from it, wait for a pick).
Boss work (ROADMAP K section) on branch `claude/boss-fights` (from TESTver, merged to TESTver and main). K1 done:
bossFight.js parry/revive fire on touch DOWN (`press`; they used to wait for the finger to lift, ~260 ms, so the bullet
had already hit), a press with nothing in reach opens a .22 s parry window (`PARRY_WIN`, whiff → .25 s lock), parry
reach 44 (was 34), revive reach 84 (was 62) and staying on the bubble .6 s revives too (`stepAssist`, `revT` ring);
tap-release only fires the SUPER now.
K2 done there: difficulty tiers `BOSS_TIERS` (data/bosses.js: Easy = old fights exactly, Medium, Hard, Extreme; multiply
the K table + boss HP; Hard/Extreme = 4 phases with a FINAL PHASE at 25%, `deck()` remixes one next-phase attack in,
`combo()` = extra light bullet volley (aimed/fan/ring/rain/bounce only, via `FC` = F with lighter k and no poses) on top of
a main attack). Engine reads `s.difficulty`, set by `requestGame({difficulty})`; HUD tag + PHASE x/N; body art gets
phase ≤2. `tests/bossdiff.mjs` now takes DIFF=0,1,2,3 and SHAMB_URL. Boss faces (`A.face`) = Broforce `headCanvas`
cut-out heads (no circle; `A.portrait` hides the bust/brute skin oval), moods via `B.mood/moodUntil`.
K3 done there: `bossTierFor(run,realm)` (bossGate.js): mind 0, body 1, spirit 2, horror 3; warp boss = `run.warp.realm`
(stored on entry; old saves: realm of the main-board return node); else levels cleared. `startBoss` passes it to
`requestGame({difficulty})` and shows it on the intro card. Online: launcher → `hostGame(game,{difficulty})` → 'game'
msg `difficulty` → `boardDuel(...,difficulty)` → duel `start` → hooks.start duel.difficulty → requestGame on both
phones. Checked: board walk Mind/Body/Spirit = Easy/Medium/Hard, rule cases in Node; online not run (no wrangler).
K5-K7 done there: `profile.bossTiers` {bossId: 0..3 highest cleared} (validated in profile.js; a boss beaten before
tiers counts as Easy), written by `recordBossTier` in gameLauncher (settleGame boss win, settleSolo arcade + online-board
guest; `curDifficulty` = tier of the launch); boss character recruit only when origin==='board' (K6). Arcade boss cards:
`arcadeLaunch` → `chooseBossTier` dialog (next tier primary, higher ones locked), medals `.boss-medals`, `.boss-extreme`
gold frame (theme.css end), result text "MEDIUM CLEARED! Hard is now open in Boss Select." Online arcade boss
challenges still Easy (proposeDuel has no tier).
K20/K15 (explosions + phases) there: `engines/bossBoom.js` = fx kinds fb/sm/db/sw/ray/em/hd in bossFight's `fx` list
(`stepBoom`/`drawBoom`, `delay` lays out sequences) + `KO_STYLE` per tier (chain secs/rate, main blast size or whole
screen, shocks, debris, embers, after-blasts, white flash, hold, title KNOCKOUT!/DESTROYED!/ANNIHILATED!!); the boss's
cut-out head tumbles off. Phase start = `phaseBreak`; phases 2+ smoke/burn (`dmgSmokeT`), aura behind the boss and edge
tint per phase, final-phase embers. fx capped at 1100.
K21 (Star Fox talk) there: `engines/bossComms.js` `createComms({blip,headFor})` = queued comm box (zip open, head in a
static screen, typed text 42 cps, square/triangle blip voice per syllable, tap skip), lines in `data/bossTalk.js`.
bossFight: `openerTalk` at fight start (SAME_* if a player IS the boss, RELATED_* if a player's character shares the
boss's `source`, else an opener), `phaseTalk(n)` = boss phaseLines[n] (or pools) + a player reply, picked from s.seed so
both phones match; phase mode waits for it (max 7 s); `chat(kind,i)` event lines (hit/down/revived/parry/super, 8 s
cooldown, quiet unless 'down'); win line on KO, boss gloat on a loss. `s.debug.comms`.
K17-K19 there: bossAttacks.js `ambush` (boss leaves the top, warned lane, bursts up from the bottom or dashes in from a
side with deadR + shots; `F.place` teleports), `bombs` (lobbed `kind:'bomb'` bullets with `ghost:true` = harmless in the
air, `lobBombStep` lands on a warned circle: blast hazard + shrapnel + `F.boom` fireball), `carpet` (swoop + a band of
bombs). `deck()` adds ambush from phase 2 (Medium+: from phase 1), bombs from phase 3 (Medium+: phase 2), carpet in the
last phase. Themed ammo `AMMO` (data/bosses.js) draws plain orbs as an emoji per boss (pink stays pink). Hit flash on the
boss toned down (it washed the boss out). `s.debug.attack(name,params)` forces one attack. Bot hits per full fight
≈ 5.4 / 7.5 / 9.3 / 14.9 by tier.
Testing on the Windows laptop: the 346 MB one-file build hangs headless Chromium; build `--pwa` into the scratchpad,
serve it (tiny node static server) and run tests with SHAMB_URL=http://localhost:8123/.

(scratch calibrate: big -19, mid -21, normal -23, small -26 dBFS max-50ms-RMS; songs ≈ -17).
Round 3 (owner): solos only for drain/super/game over (in play: generic FX + pick scrapes); songs change only when the
game turns heavy (mode/multiball start: cut to heavy) or calms (8 s later: back to the interrupted song where it was) or
a song ends (next from its start); no hot-streak/heat, no drift switching. `sfxLevel` .7 on the whole bus (-3 dB).
Round 4 (owner): levels = 1 chill (title, wait/bonus, game over), 2 energetic (play), 3 crazy (mode/multiball); sections
re-sorted (a section may list two levels); on a level change the director goes to `mem.left[L]` (song last played at that
level) where it was, else (crazy) a later crazy part of the same song, else choose(); 3→2 waits 8 s.
Round 5 (owner: "too chill", chill kept coming back, silence at ball start): chill only at game over, play = the shred,
crazy = their craziest parts; one pooled audio element per song (`POOL`, kept all session; a switch was a new 4-11 MB
element = seconds of silence on a phone), a same-song jump dips 0.3 s and seeks.
Note: generic duel chip `liveScore` reads `s.score[0]` (wrong seat for native games on seat 2); untouched.

v0.72.1 (Nov 1): v0.72.0's release stopped on Cloudflare's 25 MiB per-file limit (the one 25.3 MB built-in art script);
build.mjs --pwa now writes it as ~8 MB parts (`window.__SC_ARTN` scripts, each concats onto `__SC_ART`; #boot says "(2 of 4)")
and throws if any app file is over 24 MiB. Owner: "merging to main" = release (bump, release build, check the site serves it).
Screen wake lock while the app is visible (`src/wakeLock.js`, re-asked on return/tap). Pro Mancala: no text on the deck
(trick string/banner/"IS THINKING" in `lay.side[p]` = the player's strip (flat) or column (upright); popups weigh deck overlap
x60 and drop if still on it; `s.debug.overlays()` + tests/promancala.mjs fail on any overlap); deck lies flat whenever that
makes it longer (open Fold 900x1000 now flat); fullBleed bg dark for the title, sky/concrete gradient in play (Fold hairlines).
v0.72.0 (Nov 1, never published): the split delivery below released to main, plus a real loading-screen progress: sw.js (v4) streams the
game page and the built-in art script through `counted()` and posts {type:'sc-load',kind:'page'|'art',got,total,done} to
the loading page; index.template.html's #boot shows "Downloading vX · 45%" (MB when the size is unknown). WAIT for the
network before playing the saved copy is 2.5 s. Measured at 4x CPU: title screen 3 s per open (one-file build 9-10 s).
Nov 1 (branch `claude/pwa-split` from TESTver): installed-app updates download only what changed
(docs/PWA_SPLIT_DELIVERY.md, owner-approved, everything downloaded at install). `build.mjs --pwa` = index.html 8.2 MB +
`a/` 2,430 content-hashed files (byte-identical copies; built-in art of art.js/faceArt.js as one `a/<hash>.js` data-URI
script) + main's fixed network-first sw.js (now SW_VERSION 3): the page sends its file list (`APP_FILES` in pwa.js,
'sc-files') and the worker fills the shared `sc-assets` cache with only the missing files (resumes, keeps the newest two
versions' files, byte ranges for audio). One-file build unchanged (byte-identical). Update measured 8.3 MB (was ~200 MB). Hosting: the Worker's static assets
(`[assets]` public/, filled by `tools/worker-assets.sh` from the newest releases' `_pwa.zip`); release-build.yml deploys
the Worker with them before publishing (needs the Cloudflare secrets; release also uploads `index.html`), deploy-worker.yml
rebuilds the set. Worker `/` = release `index.html` when present. Local `wrangler dev` served the static assets right
(no 206 for ranges there: the service worker does ranges); real Cloudflare deploy, real phones and iOS Safari songs on a
first, not-yet-cached visit untested.

Version 0.70.1 (Oct 30): 79 newly pictured characters, additional moods/replacements; portrait files encoded as 480px WebP q88 with originals archived. No game code changes.
Nov 1 (branch `claude/pro-mancala`, from TESTver, unreleased): Mancala overhauled into TONY HAWK'S PRO MANCALA (same id
`greed-island-mancala`, engine name `mancala` → `engines/proMancala.js`; title/source in the engine title table in gameRegistry;
`noBleed`). Rules/CPU/online/hot-seat kept (strategyRules mancalaMove, cpuBoards pickMancala, classicBoards `netTurns`, now
exported); old `mancala()` in classicBoards unused. Owner's design: title sequence every time (Tony… Hawk's… Pro… Mancala…
EXTREME EDITION!!, tap skips, then face-off cards), THPS2-dead-on screen: skateboard-deck board (`proMancalaArt.js`
deckGeometry/makeDeck: 7-ply rings in the routed bowls, stores in the kicktails, grip, bolts; view 'side' = one wheel per
truck under the long edge, 'end' = near truck's two wheels under the tail), glass gems. Board is the focus (owner, Nov 1
round 2): `layout()` fills ~93% of the width when wide (P1 bottom row/right store) and stands it upright when tall (H>1.08W;
deck turned 90°: P1 left column + bottom store, P2 right column + top store; `pitPos` maps deck-local → screen). Skaters
are NOT on screen all the time: `cutTo(p,kind)` = full-screen 90s fisheye camcorder cutaways (`A.fisheye`/`A.vhs`; styles
follow/low/punch/orbit, crash cam on slams with freeze + record scratch, slow-mo special) for store landings, steals (grind
then the victim's slam), bails, specials and ~40% of other moves; tap skips. Spots (Nov 1 round 3): the halfpipe or
a street spot from `proMancalaSpots.js` (handrail down an 8-set, the 8-set, a red parking-lot curb, a kicker; `buildLine`
= timed roll/air/grind/slam parts, `lineState`, `drawSpot`), picked per cut kind (`SPOTS` in proMancala.js), never twice
in a row. Popups are placed where they cover no other text (`textRects`/`pop`, small ones dropped if nowhere is free, big
ones push small ones out). A ⤢ button sits left of Pause/Exit when fullscreen is available (fullBleed's bottom one hidden).
Round 4 (Nov 1): owner's sound set = Dreamcast THPS rip (named files; picker page built by a scratch script into
local-test-reports/, gitignored), installed as `assets/music/thps-sfx-<name>-<n>.wav` (random pick per play, `pickDrop`;
hooks aww/grunt/timeup/total/grind-pipe|curb|board). Songs: 15 THPS2-soundtrack MP3s from the owner as
`thps-mancala-<band>--<song>.mp3` (name shown on Now Playing; one shuffled playlist from the title on, next song when one
ends, kept across games; pauses with the game). Build is ~190 MB with them (+60 MB). Cutaways: the camera centres the
skater's body, random hats (`A.hatFor`: backwards cap, trucker, beanie, helmet; guest skaters too), air styles on store
landings (airwalk/christ/rocket via pose.feet/boardHeld), and a pose shot after most landed cuts (`startPose`: jump cut to
the skater facing the camera, `A.drawSkaterFront` horns/point/arms/shaka/flex/boardup + caption).
Round 5 (Nov 1): title words are measured on the device (Android has no Impact; wider fallback font overlapped) and
stacked above the face-off cards; `layout()` reserves a `hudH` strip for each score panel so nothing sits on the bowls
(checked at 11 sizes with a scratch script: HUD/card rects vs `lay.deckRect`); while a human chooses a bowl, trick text,
popups and the turn banner over the board fade to ~30% (`veil`, `chooseK`). Many more GOOD/BAD popup lines. Grinds in
cutaways: `GRIND_POSE` (50-50, 5-0, nose, crook, smith, feeble, board, blunt, tail; from the steal's grind name via
`grindKind`) puts a truck (or the deck for slides) on the rail/curb/coping (`grindPose`); boardslides draw the board
nose-on (`pose.boardEnd`). `s.debug.cutTo(p,kind,anim,spot)` forces a cutaway for tests. Guest roster skaters go by every 3.5-7 s
(ollie over, grind the edge, manual, bomb, big air). Turn: row glow in the player's color, "▶ X'S TURN" HUD, YOUR TURN card,
other side dimmed. Popups are measured and clamped on screen; trick strings wrap to 2 lines. THPS HUD (score tally, special meter),
trick strings "A + B / pts X n", COMBO!!! on store landings, bails (being robbed, or gaining nothing and leaving the
other a 3+ grab: combo lost), special trick when the meter is full, crude popups (owner OK'd all of them), end-of-run tally
(`profile.proMancala` {high,combo}, validated in profile.js). Trick points are cosmetic; stores decide the winner. Local 2P:
the per-turn pass card is now an in-canvas "X'S RUN" banner (+0.55 s tap lock). Sound `proMancalaAudio.js`: synth skate SFX (+catch,
revert, scratch, rollby, cam, gang "HEY!") and its own punk band `createPunk` (double-tracked panned power chords, picked
bass, skate-beat/backbeat drums with snare-roll fills, chorus lead, gang shouts; 3 original songs 216-240 bpm, sections
V/P/C/B); title = snare-roll crescendo + guitar hit per word exploding into a chorus at 4.55 s, words follow the audio clock.
Not heard by ears yet. Drop-ins `assets/music/thps-*` (README there). Test:
`tests/promancala.mjs` (2P + 1P vs CPU, 3 sizes). Online two-phone test not run (no wrangler on the Windows box).

v0.69.3 Oct 29: installed-app updates (owner: "takes really long to update"). Causes: the browser only checks sw.js on a
fresh open (Android resumes a kept app without one), the update is a ~100 MB background download that restarts from 0 if the app
is closed part-way, and the old toast said "close and reopen". Now src/pwa.js asks `reg.update()` whenever the app comes back on
screen (max every 5 min), Settings has "Version X · Check for updates" (`checkForUpdate`: /version vs VERSION, then reg.update;
states current/downloading/pending/ready/reload/offline/file; `checkUpdateFlow` in app.js), and a finished update shows a
Restart pill `#sc-ready` above the home dock (hidden while store.session). Test: `tests/pwa-update.mjs` (after build --pwa).
Not done: the Worker stores a new release fully in its cache before sending the first byte (`releaseHtml`), so the first phone
per Cloudflare location after a release waits at 0% while it pulls ~100 MB from GitHub; no resume for a half-done download.

made both P1 (each phone saw itself as Mario/Sonic, both ran the shared world). duel.js now passes `slot` (the pick, or the room
seat when both picked the same); netGame plays players[slot] with each player's real owner. Test: online-native `SAME_WHO=1`.
Lag check (same day): code adds no delay vs v0.68.1 (partner shown ~90-110 ms behind with no network, both builds, headless).
Ideas if wanted: stamp snapshots + extrapolate the partner by the full travel time, push a snapshot on jump/turn, 30 Hz.

for one player): net.js join retries + an error card that names the cause (offline / site unreachable / WebSocket refused by a
blocker) with a connection log, close 4000 = seat taken by a duplicated tab (no reconnect loop, new id), worker frees seats silent
60 s, netPlay acks {k:'npa'} (reconnect resends only unconfirmed moves), duel 20 s grace on a drop, queued rematch, version check
before challenges; per-game sync audit (Broforce/Mario/Sonic/CV/SotN/Warriors/horde/bosses/Photo Hunt/kart/board game: shared

fixed; home screen tidy + About card. Live-room probe: Actions → "Probe the online rooms" (tools/net-probe*.mjs, tools/cf-report.mjs).
Server checked healthy and far under the free-plan limits; the owner's "could not join" cause is still unknown (the new card will say).
